Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in modeling theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 831 and the invariant I(A,B) = 679 mod 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on modeling structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 831. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 679. QED.
